    89. **DoD 4: hybrid of replays and depth reprojection, second queue (evidence: subagent; quiet window, clinic with camera pan, each the last 8 s; `replay_check.py log=` for replays and warps; validation with warp and second queue; OFF gate solo; own visual check of `build/warp_triple.png` and `build/warp_disocc.png`):** Camera from the 864-B block: VP = P (dwords 52–67) · V (dwords 8–19), row-wise, D3D depth with far = 1 (checked offline: dwords 20–35 = (P·V)⁻¹, 36–51 = P⁻¹), each dword interpolated as for the replays (`present.cpp` `camera_at`). Warp (`warp_one`): forward warp as grid mesh with 2-px cells and depth test; at disocclusion (ratio of inverse depths > 1.15) the cell takes the farthest corner (background fills), the image edge is backed with the source image. HUD: the source of the display pass (non-blended draw into the display target, largest sampled texture, then 1025×1 LUT) is detected automatically; `scene` = copy before the first blended draw without RT sampling (bloom counts as scene), `pre` = after the HUD; if a pixel is HUD at the target or at the source position, the unwarped source image is used (+8 MB per snapshot). Every present without its own rendered image shows the nearest image warped to its alpha. Second queue of the same family (priority 1.0 vs 0.5; only with `BB_INTERP=2`, ≥ 2 queues, not with `BB_PRESENT_QUEUE=0`): replay submit in batches per image with timeline semaphore, the presenter takes only finished images and presents on queue 2. Switches `BB_INTERP_WARP`, `BB_INTERP_WARP_CELL`, `BB_PRESENT_QUEUE` (README). **Measurement:** 165 Hz with queue 2: 165 presents/s, ≈ 160–165 distinct images/s, interval p50/p95/p99 6.06/6.06/6.20 ms, ≈ 98 warps/s, 2.5 images per tick; without queue 2: p95 7.9 (up to 15.2), p99 11.4 (up to 18.8) ms; `refresh` (FIFO, 165 Hz) with queue 2: 165.0 presents/s without missed vsyncs, 158 distinct images/s, p99 7.34 ms; without: 149.7/s, p95 16.0 ms. GPU: warp 0.20–0.22 ms (1-px cells 1.3–1.6 ms), replay 7.9–8.7 ms, render thread 2.0–2.5 ms CPU per tick. Acceptance criterion (165 Hz, pan): 10 warp images tile shift minus alpha median +0.002, 10/10 within ±0.1 (figure 9/10), replays 10/10; 1-px cells 11/11, 60 Hz 8/8; HUD pixels 99.88–100 % within 2/255 of the source. Own visual check: intermediate image geometrically correct between both render images; at silhouettes 2-px staircases and ≤ 6 px stretched background fringe (visible in still image). Drops to 31–60 distinct images/s (83) explained: under 4–5 other games the replay GPU measured 27–415 ms, the budget fell to 1 image per tick – with warps it now stays at ≈ 160–165 distinct images/s, independent of replay count. Gates: build without warnings, ctest 8/8, validation (warp + queue 2): only 06479 and 09600 from the known texture with base 0 (784×662), now reported per replay batch; four previously found presenter `09600` (warp from a packet without warp data) fixed; OFF gate solo: `BB_WW_VERIFY` 0 wrong, screenshot 14.02 – **but** an earlier OFF run in parallel with another game reported 4 vertex deviations at `0x10e971ffcc..d8` (not reproduced solo; investigation ongoing). Open: disocclusion staircases, figures/animation in the warp only at replay pace (only the camera is reprojected), transparency/particles with the depth of the surface behind, HUD detection heuristic, camera cuts without special handling, +≈ 190 MB for `pre` images, benefit of the second queue driver-dependent (only NVIDIA measured).
        Addendum: the 4 vertex deviations of the OFF run under load are late guest writes after the submit's poll, not a write-watch error – see 93.
    90. **`exp pos1–3`, `image_sample_*_o`, cause of the green glow narrowed down (evidence: subagent; unit tests in `test_spirv` with SPIR-V validation; Dream runs; pixel history `BB_PIXEL`, shader probe `BB_SPV_PROBE`, texture dump `BB_TEX_DUMP` with decoding in Python):** (1) `exp pos1–3` (target 13–15) per `PA_CL_VS_OUT_CNTL`: misc vector (bit 21; only x as `PointSize` with `USE_VTX_POINT_SIZE`, edge flag/RT/viewport index ignored, ponytail), CCDIST0/1 (bits 22/23) to `ClipDistance`/`CullDistance` via `CLIP_DIST_ENA_i`/`CULL_DIST_ENA_i`; if nothing is enabled, the export is ignored as on hardware – shaders are never discarded anymore. `GcnEnv::vs_out_cntl`, in the VS key; `shaderClipDistance`/`shaderCullDistance` enabled. (2) All `image_sample_*_o` variants: offset VGPR (6-bit fields, signed) shifts the coordinates by offset/size of the sampled level (Vulkan allows non-constant offsets only for gather); ponytail: nearest level, bias/`_d` for level selection ignored, offsets on cubemaps reported as error. (3) Also corrected (no visual effect on the green): `v_mul/mac/mad_legacy_f32` with 0·x = 0 also for inf/NaN, `v_cvt_pkrtz_f16_f32` exact toward zero (finite above 65504), buffer dfmt 6/7 channel order (R in the low bits). **Green:** does not arise in particle draws, but in the deferred-lighting composite `ps@10d5fd4000` from G-buffer MRT5 (`1078ce0000`, RGBA16F, written by the terrain PS `ps@10d836fd00`: G = 4.18 with R/B ≈ 0); there two `image_sample_l` on a **BC6H cubemap** (T# `10e5a8bb00`, 128², tiling 13, 8 levels) deliver the green term. Dump: level 0 plausible; in levels 1–3, instead of BC6H blocks, there are RGBA32F texels; level 7 decodes to pure green – the mip data come from wrong offsets or are written by the GPU at runtime ([INFERENCE] addrlib pads cubemap slices from mip 1 up to a power of two, 6 → 8; `mip_chain` does not). Counter-check BC6H as SFLOAT: green gone, everything white-blue (reverted). [INFERENCE] The lamp octagon follows from the same too-bright green in the bloom chain (not checked via pixel history). Gates: build without warnings, ctest 8/8; validation, `BB_WW_VERIFY` and clinic screenshot for this status still open. Next: cubemap mip layout.
        Continuation: `BB_TEX_DUMP` now also writes the raw guest bytes (`.raw`). `tiling::mip_chain` pads the slices of arrays and cubemaps from level 1 on to a power of two (cube 6 → 8, like addrlib `PostComputeMipLevel`/`PadDimensions` without `noCubeMipSlicesPad`; volume textures unchanged; test case in `check_mips`: 128² BC6H, 1D thin, level 1 at 98,304, level 2 at 131,072). Raw data of `10e5a8bb00`: 0–98,304 valid BC6H, 98,304–131,072 RGBA32F texels (exactly level 1 with 8 slices), from 131,072 valid BC6H again (level 2 with padding; without it, it would lie in the middle of the float data), from level 5 float data again; `BB_GPU_WATCH` (only DMA/copies) saw no writer. Image with padding worse: the player character also green (`build/fx_dream_cubepad.png`). [INFERENCE] Level 1 and 5+ are produced by the game at runtime or they come from another source. Gates for this status (clinic screenshot, validation, `BB_WW_VERIFY`) open; investigation of the writer ongoing.
        **Fixed (evidence: subagent; Dream run with extended `BB_GPU_WATCH` over `[10e5a8bb00, +0x34000)` and new `BB_HWWATCH` – hardware debug registers DR0–DR3, `BB_HWWATCH_ALL=1` with guest backtrace; clinic runs with RT dump, `BB_PIXEL`, `BB_SPV_PROBE`, `BB_RES_DUMP`; own visual check `build/cube_dream.png`, `build/cube_sick.png`):** two separate causes. (a) **Cubemap layout:** no GPU writer; a single guest `memcpy` (caller `0x2173150`) copies 0x34000 = 212,992 bytes from the file buffer – the chain comes complete from the file. The size only fits if **every** level, including level 0, occupies 8 instead of 6 cube slots (addrlib `PadDimensions`, `NextPow2(slices)` for cubes); the RGBA32F "texels" lie 100 % in slots 6–7 and in micro-tile fill areas (fill material from the file), in the used BC6H blocks 0 %. `tiling::mip_chain(…, cube)`: cubes get 8 slots on all levels, arrays from level 1 on, or with POW2_PAD; reading goes to the end of the last used slice; tests in `check_mips` (cube: level 1 at 131,072, level 2 at 163,840). (b) **Translator error with if/else:** the lamp octagon came from the additive particle draw `vs@10d95d5100`/`ps@10d966f200` (vertex color `[255,123,123,255]` correct, fetch and input assignment correct), whose PS branches via `s_cmp_eq_i32 s0, 2; s_cbranch_scc0 ELSE; …; s_branch END; ELSE: …; END:` – the closing `s_branch` of the predicated then region skipped the else branch for all lanes (here the normal blend path). A predicated then region that ends with a forward `s_branch` over the else region is now translated as if/else (from ELSE exec = saved & !cond, restored at END); new test in `test_spirv`. **Image:** Dream – slope, glow points and player character no longer green, the doll is visible; clinic lamp – softer, pale-blue glow, the messenger beings are visible (octagon crop RGB before 225/245/130, after 29/33/45). Extended (off): `BB_GPU_WATCH` also reports sampled/storage T#s, RTs and uploads over the range; `BB_RES_DUMP` names each draw; `BB_SHADER_DIR` with fetch shader. Gates: build without warnings, ctest 8/8; clinic gate, validation and `BB_WW_VERIFY` after both fixes still open. Side finding, not changed: `get_shader` checks the fetch shader at the address of the first translation, not at the one from the user data of the current draw – a VS with a different fetch shader would get the old register assignment (not observed in the clinic).
    91. **DoD 5, stage 2: internal resolution `BB_RES_SCALE=<1..3>` (evidence: subagent; runs individually per the run rule; s=1 validation + `BB_WW_VERIFY` 416 s; s=2 validation 300 s; `BB_GPU_TS`; s=2 downscaled to 1080p per 2×2 box against s=1 at the same pad clock; own visual check of `build/dream_crops.png`):** `res_scale()` (integer only; < 148 bytes push constants → fallback to 1 with log line); `Image::scale`, `w`/`h` physical, `gw()`/`gh()` guest size; `get_rt`/`get_ds` scale screen-sized single-layer targets (16:9 ± 5 %, width ≥ 960) – shadow maps, cubes, glare pyramid stay native; if a draw binds differently scaled targets, the native one is re-created once scaled (in the game, once: depth `0x106da20000` 1024×640). Viewport, scissor, render area and depth-clear rectangle × s; crops/direct binding of padded targets with guest sizes; fill helpers in guest bytes (partial fills with zeros piecewise); snapshots, display/replay blit, warp depth copy in physical pixels. Shader: only for s > 1, a dword with scaling bits follows the 16 user dwords (bit i = resource i scaled, bit 31 = targets scaled; push layout VS/CS 0–68, PS 80–148; at s = 1 layout and SPIR-V stay unchanged); POS_X/Y return guest pixels for scaled targets (FragCoord/s), `ds_swizzle` stays physical; padding correction with T#·s, texture offsets in guest texels; `image_load` on scaled images reads `c·s + (FragCoord mod s)` (covers the display pass `ps@10d5e00f00`), `image_store` in CS writes the s×s block (also `cs@1059603800`), in PS the own sub-pixel. Test in `test_spirv` (s = 2). **Result:** s = 2 renders clinic and Hunter's Dream internally at 3840×2160 with bloom, depth of field, fog/sky glow, chromatic aberration, tone mapping and HUD, matching; downscaled against native: 0.33 % (clinic) resp. 3.7 % (Dream, only edges and foliage; `build/dream_diff.png`) of pixels > 24/255, offset 0/0. GPU per frame s = 2: 28.5 ms (screen-sized passes 15.2, copies 1.9, barriers 3.2) vs 12.2 ms at 1080p; ≈ 27 images/s while a second game ran. Gates: s = 1 validation only 06479/09600, `BB_WW_VERIFY` 7.14 million 0 wrong, textures 21,049 checks 0 stale, clinic screenshot 14.49; s = 2 validation only 06479/09600.     92. **Installer hardened; last stub imports replaced (evidence: own tests with a mini dump from files of the 1.09 folder – `eboot.bin`, one data file, `param.sfo`, `icon0.png` –, `bbinstall hash` + `install`; ctest 8/8; clinic run):** `bbinstall install` checks the free space at the target (nearest existing ancestor) against the size of the trees to copy before copying and aborts with "Not enough free space … need X GiB, Y GiB available"; writes `<target>/.bbinstall-incomplete` before copying and removes it only after success (an aborted run is reported the next time: "A previous install … did not finish; copying again"); after copying, checks the **installed** copy against the manifest (manifest paths mapped to the install layout: `Image0/…` → `game/…`, `Sc0/param.sfo|icon0.png|trophy/` → `game/sce_sys/…`, non-installed license files dropped) – mini dump: source 4/4 and copy 4/4 verified, marker removed; non-writable target: "Cannot write to … (permissions?)", exit 1; failed individual copies name the file and the error text. Lack of space and copy errors themselves were not reproduced. HLE libc: the four imports last bridged via `BB_STUB_IMPORTS` are implemented – `std::ios_base::Init::Init()` and `std::_Winit::_Winit()` as empty constructors (the game outputs nothing via iostreams), `_Locksyslock`/`_Unlocksyslock` as 8 recursive locks like Dinkumware (`_MAX_LOCK` 8).

Every deviation is re-polled immediately: if the write watch now reports the pages as written, a guest thread wrote **after** this submit's poll ("late write"); if they stay clean, the watch would have missed a write ("blind") – result 4 of 4 "late", 0 "blind"; with `BB_PAD_CLOCK=flips` twice at the same game image 4473 (once in each arm) – [INFERENCE] a fixed game event rewrites a dynamic vertex buffer while we process the submit. The cause is the known window "one poll per submit" (under load up to 100 ms old, 27.1 million such reuses in 300 s), not the page table from 87; bisect: default 5 events/≈ 128 million, `BB_TEX_WW=0` 1/≈ 75 million. Consequence: on the PS4 the GPU reads only at execution – such late writes it sees there [INFERENCE] depending on timing; for us the state at recording time counts.     96. **SCC branches as real selection; fetch shader per draw (evidence: subagent; `BB_SCALE_LOG`, per-draw dump, `BB_SKIP_PS` now with a list; before/after clinic at same pad clock; Dream run; Gates):** the three VS from 95 and four more (`s_buffer_load: V# is not a user-data/s_load descriptor`, from the Dream) were a **regression of the if/else fix from 90**: in `s_cbranch_scc1 ELSE; THEN: s_buffer_load_dwordx8 s[0:7] …; s_branch END; ELSE: s_load_dwordx4 s[0:3], s[6:7] …` the else branch was translated with the register state after the then branch (s[6:7] already overwritten); at runtime the predicated then region always ran along, and its scalar writes are not exec-masked. Now forward `s_cbranch_scc0/1` are translated as structured selection (`OpSelectionMerge` + `OpBranchConditional`, SCC is wave-uniform); the else branch starts with the register knowledge from the branch point, at the merge only shared knowledge remains, and differing symbolic s_load results are written into the SGPR per path (`flush_loads`); test `if/else scalar state` in `test_spirv` (fails without the fix with exactly that message). The affected VS skin the player character into the half-resolution motion-vector buffer `106dd50000` (960×540, for motion blur) or into the 4096² shadow map and a 256×256 target; in the color image only edges on the character change (0.1 % of pixels; `build/vst_*.png`). **Fetch shader:** `Translation` records the origin of the `s_swappc_b64` address (`fetch_lo`/`fetch_hi`), `get_shader` compares the fetch code at the address from the user data of the current draw and re-translates on differing code; log line once per VS and address. Finding: in > 1000 s clinic and in the Dream no draw used a different fetch shader (note from 90 closed). Gates: build without warnings, ctest 8/8, validation clinic 300 s only 06479/09600, `BB_WW_VERIFY` 275 s no deviation line (without summary line), clinic screenshot normal (13.52), Dream 200 s without abort, no "not translated" left except the known `ps@105b20fa00` (translated later).

   100. **Rainbow fur fixed – blood maps of the characters (evidence: subagent; fight from `sickroom` with absolute pad script and `BB_PAD_CLOCK=flips`; G-buffer dump `build/rb_v6.png`, `BB_PIXEL`, `BB_CONST_DUMP`, `BB_GPU_WATCH`; before/after `build/rb_beast_before_after.png`, sequence `build/rb_sheet12.png`):** the character PS `ps_e21e283a` (`ps@10d7eccf00`/`10d7e73200`/`10d7ef5400`) blends the albedo via second UVs with two blood maps (T# at table offset 0x148 RGBA8 sRGB and 0x150 RGBA8 UNORM, factor from s1). After a hit the game renders a 512² blood map per character; a generic copy CS (`cs@10d5e00300`, `image_load_mip` → `image_store`) writes it via a UINT storage T# mip by mip (0…9) to RT base + 1 MB; the PS there reads sRGB with 10 mips. Three backend errors: (1) `get_texture` keyed with nfmt – storage UINT and sample sRGB of the same address were two VkImages, and the sampled one came from stale guest memory (zeros → black, old foreign data → rainbow from 97), and the storage image had only 1 mip; (2) `image_load` read the RT as UINT through an sRGB/UNORM view (type mismatch); (3) the Gnm fill helper fills on the CPU before an RT exists there – the new RT started at 0 instead of the fill color (alpha 0 = full blood, color 0 → black character). Fix in `src/gpu/backend.cpp`: uncompressed textures without nfmt keyed and `MUTABLE_FORMAT` (also RTs), storage T# with full mip chain, view format = T# format within the same dfmt family (storage via the UNORM twin, view key with format, otherwise `VkImageViewUsageCreateInfo`), fill color remembered per address and used as clear when the RT is created later (CMASK fast clear takes precedence). Limits (ponytail): an image first created by a sample T# without mips stays single-level; for sRGB targets only fill values 0 and 1 are exact. Diagnostics `BB_OPS_LOG`, `BB_GPU_STATS`, `BB_PIXEL_AFTER` now also on the pad time (`p<s>`). Gates: build without warnings, ctest 8/8, `BB_WW_VERIFY` clinic 290 s 8,050,962 checks 0 wrong / 24,330 textures 0 stale, validation 300 s only 06479 ×20 / 09600 ×2, clinic screenshot 13.44. Not checked: validation in combat.
       - **Addendum 100:** `destroy_image` now also removes the associated entries from `Backend::views` (previously a leak and possible collision when a VkImage handle was reused; ponytail: linear search per destroyed image). Dream run on this build: scene, lighting and cube reflections as in `build/cube_dream.png` (comparison `build/rbdream_cmp.png`). 165 Hz (`interp_bench.py 165`, quiet slot): 165.0 presents/s, 163.7 distinct images/s, interval p99 6.16 ms – `MUTABLE_FORMAT` costs measurably nothing.

New `src/hle/avplayer.cpp` (all 16 imports, removing the NULL stubs from 104): small decoder interface, under `_WIN32` Media Foundation Source Reader (NV12 or PCM16, libraries linked only under `if(WIN32)`), otherwise still init → NULL. Struct layouts (InitData with allocator/event callbacks, StreamInfo 0x28, FrameInfoEx 0x68, audio FrameInfo 0x28, events STOP 1/READY 2/PLAY 3/PAUSE 4) taken from shadPS4 `avplayer.h` as the fact source and checked per field against the accesses in the game code (`f_290e0e0`, `f_290f9c0`, `f_290eae0`, `f_2910710`). Video frames in GPU-visible memory obtained by the game via allocator (NV12, chroma at align64(width)·height, ring of 2 frames; the write watch detects new frames), paced by wall clock, frames > 50 ms late discarded; audio 5.1 → stereo (ITU, without LFE). READY arrives asynchronously 100 ms after AddSource from a guest thread (`start_guest_thread` from `thread.cpp`), because the handler needs the game's audio thread. `sceKernelMprotect` as a no-op returning 0 (the game's allocator calls it for GPU access). Result title attract: complete, `ended t=163778 ms video 4906 frames (1 dropped, decode avg 279 us) audio … 7859200 samples` (= 163.7 s audio), colors and aspect ratio as in the reference image, then back in the title menu; cross at 225 s → Stop, Close, title menu. Limits (ponytail): no seeking, no subtitles, always stereo downmix, fixed values (READY 100 ms, audio lead 100 ms), Mprotect does not store the protection, decoding synchronous on the game thread (≈ 0.3 ms/frame); Linux still without movies. Unchecked: opening (audio track selection) and end credits in-game. Gates: build without warnings, ctest 8/8, clinic 300 s normal.

On READY the game counts the audio streams via `GetStreamInfo` and enables the n-th with `sceAvPlayerEnableStream` (here n = 1). **Bug fixed:** Media Foundation does not deliver the streams in MP4 order (in the opening: 8 audio tracks reversed, video last; confirmed by level comparison against ffmpeg), so the wrong audio track played first. Now `dec_open` reads the track list of the MP4 (moov/trak, hdlr, stsz) and maps each MF stream to its track via type and the sizes of the first 512 samples; if the mapping does not fit: log line and MF order. Movie subtitles: English [INFERENCE: audio track 2 is English; all tracks are marked `und`]. End credits unchecked (no save at the end). Build without warnings, ctest 8/8.

   110. **"s_load: guest memory unreadable" – targeted re-translation (evidence: subagent; p7b log and code; 4 runs in the Old Yharnam hall on the current build, `build/sl1_sheet.png`; clinic gate `build/sl_gate.log`, `build/sl_gate_s.png`):** affected were embedded fetch shaders (`s_load_dwordx4 s[16:19], s[2:3]` – V# table via user SGPR 2:3, e.g. `vs@10d7e62000`) and PS constant-buffer tables (`s_load_dwordx8 s[16:23], s[2:3]`); the translator reads these tables at translation time, because stride/format/image type are baked into the SPIR-V. "Unreadable" = the table pointer of this draw lay in no known guest mapping; such a failure previously blocked up to 127 draws (retry every 128th use), even when their memory was already readable. Now `Translation::read_failed` records the failed read access; the entry is re-translated as soon as a draw can read that access, otherwise only the draw is skipped; structural errors ("descriptor/sampler unreadable") are permanent; message with address; unit test in `test_spirv`. Not reproduced on the current build (4 runs, also with `BB_RES_SCALE=3` at 10–15 fps: 0 errors, the same shaders translate when the chapel loads; p7b ran with a binary built before aa9719b). The 13–18 fps in p7b began 43 s before the first error and coincide with a parallel game run (mv_ng) (blend-pass median from 0.015 to 0.4 ms) [INFERENCE: GPU contention]; otherwise 30 fps in the same hall. Gates: build without warnings, ctest 8/8, clinic 300 s with validation only 06479 ×20 / 09600 ×2, `BB_WW_VERIFY` 4,537,967 checks 0 wrong (1 late write), 13,571 textures 0 stale, screenshot normal.

   116. **Scalar bitfield/bit instructions in the translator: `s_bfe_i32` among others (evidence: subagent; corpus `build/vst_corpus` clinic + Dream = 512 shaders, `bbspv --replay --write-spv`; target VS `vs@10d76c0900` from a Forbidden Woods run with `BB_SHADER_DIR`; unit tests in `test_spirv`):** `s_bfe_i32` new (arithmetic shift by the offset, width 0 → 0, width ≥ 32 → shift without extension, otherwise sign extension from bit width−1; generates no SCC – ponytail, as with `s_bfe_u32`); `s_bfe_u32` rewritten (width 0/32 and offset + width > 32 are now defined, previously undefined behaviour in `OpBitFieldUExtract`); also new `s_bfm_b32`, `s_orn2/nand/nor/xnor_b32`, `s_abs_i32`, `s_absdiff_i32`, `s_brev_b32`, `s_bcnt0/1_i32_b32`, `s_ff0/ff1_i32_b32`, `s_flbit_i32_b32`, `s_flbit_i32`, `s_sext_i32_i8/i16`. The tests check translation and `spirv-val` (eight literal cases plus register form), not the executed values. Corpus: 490 of 512 valid, 0 invalid, **0 previously translated shaders changed** (490 word-for-word identical), 22 fail as before (20× LDS in replay without `lds_bytes`, 2× image type 0). `vs@10d76c0900` (DS/tess, `s_bfe_i32 s1, s0, 0x180000`) now translates and is valid; `vs@10d8324f00` was not dumped separately (same pattern); the message is missing from the game log. Not yet supported: all `_b64` forms, `s_cmov_b32`, `s_cmovk_i32`, `s_addc_u32`, `s_subb_u32`, `s_bitset0/1_b32`, `s_wqm_b32`, `s_quadmask_b32`, `s_movrel*`, `saveexec` variants except `s_and_saveexec_b64`, `s_getreg/setreg`, `s_cbranch_fork/join`. Gates: build without warnings, ctest 8/8, clinic 300 s with validation only 06479 ×20 / 09600 ×2, 0 blind, 0 stale (12,408 checked); clinic screenshot mean 13.66 (single frame, slightly above 13.2–13.5, not investigated further).
       - **Limitation of 116:** the new scalar operations are tested only for translation and `spirv-val`, not for values; SCC is set by `s_bfe_*`, `s_abs_i32`, `s_absdiff_i32`, `s_bcnt*` and `s_orn2/nand/nor/xnor` in hardware (result ≠ 0), but not by these translations – a later `s_cbranch_scc*`/`s_cselect` could silently run wrong (fix commissioned).

   118. **Curtain SH = 0 is explained by the data; no translation error demonstrable (evidence: subagent; HW-watch hits, census logs `build/shp18.log` (census) and `shp8.log` (map dump), image only from before at `build/shp1_s.png`):** the 13-float4 instance table is copied via guest `memcpy` from a stack buffer into a pool (caller `f_2255960` instance loop → `f_22548c0` builds the 0xD0 record); the SH records 9–11 come from three sources: A) global default, B) object-owned probe (flag +0x170 = 1, only on probe hit), C) LightSet entry with key (map, LightSetID). The curtains (MSB objects `o240910_5500/5502/5503/5504`, no MapPieces) take C with flag 0, without probe, key (m24, 1); `f_22e0ec0` computes the SH rows of the entry every frame. The gparam file `m24_00_0000.gparambnd`, group LightSet, row ID 1 has hemi up/down = (0.5; 0.5; 0.5) with **intensity 0** (also ID 0 and 2); the SH formula (color² · w, averaged) then yields exactly 0. The entries in the run match the file rows (e.g. ID 0x18 → 0.78, ID 0x33 → 0.125): SH math and AVX operations are thereby verified against the data; the file reads are correct. Probe path: `m24_00_00_00_0000.btpb` contains 42 groups with 2 m probe grids; the hall is group 2 (y 36.5–42.5; z 318.8–354.4), the curtain pivots lie at y = 46.64, i.e. 4 m above – per the calculation in no group (consistent with flag 0). **Open:** a reference from the real game is missing (whether it looks this dark there – unverified); the probe search itself (pivot or model center, tolerance) was not decoded – the only point where a recompiler bug could still hide (a Python emulation against the BTPB data would decide); two further items at (66.8; 46.6; 319.0) with key (24, 1), flag 1 and probe set are unexplained; only BTPB 0000 is loaded (0001/0002 never requested). No code fix (an SH constant would be a hack). **New debug code:** `BB_CONST_DUMP_VS=<vs hex>` (const dump per VS), `BB_HWWATCH_VS=<vs>,<res>,<off>,<keyoff>,<keyfloat>` (runtime hardware write watch on pool blocks; hook `gpu::hooks().hw_watch`, `hw_watch_set` in `crash.cpp`, `BB_HWWATCH_ALL` shows 150 instead of 60 hits); ctest and gates for this change not yet run.
   119. **Forest drop to 4.6–10 fps fixed: LDS of large patch draws in device memory (evidence: subagent; same route `route_grave_byrgenwerth` from `forbidden_grave`; logs `build/fp1.log` (before) / `fp6.log` (after), comparison `build/fp_forest_cmp.png`; `BB_GPU_TS`, GPU log counters):** no texture, vertex cache or descriptor problem (uploads 3181 for 1669 textures, evictions 0, pool errors 0), but the GPU: 90 % busy, 96 ms/frame at 9.3 fps; the passes of the tessellation draws cost 54.7 ms (9 LS dispatches `vs@10d76a1200` at ≈ 6 ms each) plus ≈ 22 ms G-buffer. In the forest there are 6,000–25,000 patches per draw (≈ 10 draws/frame); the emulated LDS (`count·256` bytes, up to 16 MB per draw) lay in the host-visible upload ring (`AUTO_PREFER_HOST`, over PCIe) – there the fill ran, the LS wrote into it and the DS read from it. Fix in `Backend::tess_begin` (`src/gpu/backend.cpp`): from 256 KB on, the LDS lives in a device buffer with 16 MB per slot (`tess_dev_lds()`, created on first use; a draw without a barrier cannot overwrite the buffer the previous draw still reads); small LDS (clinic) stays in the ring unchanged. Measured values before → after: fps 4.6–11.8 → 30.0 throughout (also at the Byrgenwerth light, previously 9–10), route to the light 181 → 122 s, GPU busy 96.3 → 18.6 ms/frame, LS dispatches 60.8 → 1.05 ms, G-buffer pass 21.7 → 7.7 ms. **No VRAM leak:** 1.5 GB at the title, ≈ 4.2 GB after loading the map (t ≈ 75–130 s), then flat (4189 MB from 130 to 220 s); breakdown at ≈ 140 s: upload ring 1408 MB, 4 vertex pools at 256 MB each, 1595 textures 918 MB, 141 RTs 587 MB, 11 depth buffers 130 MB; new up to 128 MB for the LDS buffer. Gates: build without warnings, ctest 8/8, clinic 300 s with validation and `BB_WW_VERIFY=1` only 06479 ×20 / 09600 ×2, 0 mismatches, 0 stale, screenshot 13.41; Dream mean (53.7/53.9/49.7) against `mdream2.png` (53.5/53.7/49.6), unchanged in the montage comparison (no pixel comparison in the same exe). **Open:** the HS of the forest patches (`hs@10d76bdc00`, 3 control points, triangle domain) computes tess factors (≤ 16, frustum culling) but is not executed – we always tessellate with 16 (the G-buffer pass is more expensive than necessary); the DS grid is square, the domain a triangle (w = 1−u−v), the DS read HS data that stay empty (0) on our side – both unchecked; because of the barrier between patch draws, LS and DS no longer overlap (irrelevant at 30 fps; not measured above `BB_FPS` > 30); `BB_SHADER_DIR` crashed after ≈ 90 s with 0xC0000005 (run `fp4`, not investigated).

No crashes, no "unimplemented import", ≈ 30 fps.

   124. **Damage formula OK; in the Rom runs the fist hit (Evidence: subagent DamageCalc; temporary log in guest code of `f_1a2ab80` and hit structure, runs dc1–dc5; menu readout; measurement tools `build/dc_*.py`, logs `dc*.log`):** Measurements (target / AR in hit record / damage): Central Yharnam **without cheats**, Huntsman 70/70 (defense 70): AR 94.86 → 83 damage, one hit kills; Yharnam with godmode + `tp`, enemy 150 HP: AR 94.86/96.76 → 49/51; Rom arena from `byrgenwerth`, Rom 4235 (defense 130): AR 25.59/26.10 → 2/2; Byrgenwerth garden from `byrgenwerth`, enemy 762 HP (defense 104): AR 26.1/25.59 → ≈ 3; garden after one `press right`: AR 96.76 → 34. The curve in `f_1576460` (piecewise quadratic function, constants 0.12/1/2.5/8, reduction 90/60/30/10 %) matches the log (ratio 1.355 → 49.8 vs. 49.82; ratio 4.4 → 30.7 after multiplier 0.8578 vs. 30.7356); multipliers on player hits 1.0 (the Huntsman 1.2 from enemy state/effects); UI: equipment screen shows the Saw Cleaver with Physical ATK 90 +4 = 94, Level 10, Vit 11, End 10, Str 12, Skill 10, Blt 9, Arc 8, Durability 110/200. **Cause of the low damage:** the AR input `rec[0]` is 25.59 instead of 94.86 (constant factor 0.2698), because the AtkParam ID in the record (+0x90) in the Rom run is 100000/100010 (fists) instead of 700000/700010 (Saw Cleaver); the right hand is empty in the screenshot. Already after Continue in `savedata_byrgenwerth` the fist hits (in the garden, without a sea event and without `tp` hops); a single `press right` (D-pad = switch weapon slot) restores AR 96.76 – so the template's active right-hand slot points to an empty slot, while the inventory contains the Saw Cleaver. **Conclusion for 121:** the constant damage 2 (H4) is thus explained (fist AR), **NOHIT/GODMODE are not the cause** (damage without cheats is normal; the cheat code path writes only the player HP block and its flags, not the global `All*` bytes at 0x593e860); the statement in 121 "port error very likely" is **superseded**. **Open:** whether the empty active slot is a save/load error of the slot index or comes from a script error [INFERENCE candidate: the Exit Game scripts press `rechts` twice for the menu tabs; if the menu is not yet open, the D-pad switches the weapon slot – agent RomFinish checks this]; the templates `oedon`, `cathedral`, `grand_cathedral`, `forbidden_woods` were not checked for the weapon (only `yharnam_lamp` with Saw Cleaver and `byrgenwerth` with fist are evidenced); the telemetry `wpn=` is not built; the NOHIT/GODMODE comparison on the same enemy and the test with `play\bbgame.exe` were not done; runs dc3 to dc5 partly overlapped (beyond the one-run limit). It is possible that later boss fights (Shadows of Yharnam, Rom) were fought with fists [INFERENCE: fits ≈ 45 fight cycles]. No code changed, gates not run.
       - **Resolved by 127:** the empty right-hand slot was a script error (Exit Game script pressed `right` in the game); the slot index is saved/loaded correctly; `savedata_byrgenwerth_armed` repaired.
   125. **Scalar ALU instructions now set SCC; deterministic A/B shows only a small figure change (Evidence: subagents SBfe and SccAB; corpus `build/vst_corpus` (512 shaders), `bbspv --replay`; A/B `BB_PAD_CLOCK=flips` + `BB_SHOT_CLOCK=pad`, shots at pad second 140/170, heat maps `build/ab_diff_*.png`, figure comparison ×4 `build/ab_figure_old_new_x4.png` – own visual check: indistinguishable):** Review note confirmed: `s_and/or/xor/andn2/orn2/nand/nor/xnor_b32`, `s_not_b32`, `s_lshl/lshr/ashr`, `s_bfe_*`, `s_abs_i32`, `s_absdiff_i32`, `s_bcnt0/1` set SCC = (result ≠ 0) in hardware, `s_min/max_*` SCC = 1 if S0 is selected, `s_add_i32`/`s_sub_i32`/`s_addk_i32` SCC = signed overflow (checked against `aco_opcodes.py`); the translator wrote SCC only for `s_add_u32`, `s_sub_u32` and `s_cmp_*` – a following `s_cbranch_scc*`/`s_cselect` could read a stale value or fail with "without SCC" (also for the already existing instructions). Now set for all named instructions; `s_bfm`, `s_mul_i32`, `s_mulk`, `s_brev`, `s_ff0/ff1`, `s_flbit*`, `s_sext*` leave SCC untouched (test: a sequence with `s_cselect` must fail with "without SCC"). Corpus: 512 shaders, 490 valid, 0 invalid; 452 identical word for word, 38 changed (31 CS, 4 PS, 3 VS), only through additional SCC stores (`OpStore`, `OpINotEqual`, in 19 shaders one `OpVariable`; in 4 PS overflow operations) – checked by opcode histogram, no full word comparison. Tests check only translation and `spirv-val`, not values; unchecked instructions carry `ponytail: unexecuted`. **A/B clinic** (old = `play\bbgame.exe` from 7.10. without s_bfe commit, new = current build): noise floor old/old mean ±0.13–0.17 (pixels > 4: 1.1–1.4 %, > 16: 0.3–0.5 %; flickering light and window on the right of the image), the figure is exactly reproducible (mean deviation 0.008); old/new mean +0.41/+0.34, pixels > 4: 1.6/0.9 %, > 16: 0.17/0.15 %; in addition to the noise, a real, deterministic change to the figure (hood, cape, edges; mean deviation ≈ 3.1) and to the lattice work at top center (bounding box ≈ 820–1080 × 400–700), visibly the same; **with the SCC store switched off (temporary switch, removed) the figure already deviates from old by 3.3–3.5** – the change therefore comes from the `s_bfe` commit or from other differences in the exes (forest LDS, sload fix); SCC on/off shifts the figure by a further ≈ 1.6; attribution to individual shaders not done. Dream not deterministic (pad clock does not hold the scene still, no old/old run) – no statement there. Verdict: the mean drift 12.94 → 13.89 is predominantly noise; the small figure change is evidenced neither as a fix nor as a regression [INFERENCE: the `s_bfe_u32` edge case (width 32 / offset + width > 32) was previously undefined]. Build without warnings, ctest 8/8, clinic 300 s with validation only 06479 ×20 / 09600 ×2, 0 blind, 0 stale (14,490 checked). **Consequence for the gates:** the clinic mean ≈ 13.4 is too coarse as a gate; better a pad-clock shot against a reference (pixel share, bounding box).
       - **Correction to 125 (attribution of the figure change):** the hypothesis "`s_bfe_u32` edge case" is to be struck – the corpus (116) showed for the `s_bfe` commit all 490 previously translated shaders identical word for word, including those with `s_bfe_u32`; this commit cannot have changed the figure. The change is deterministic and **not attributed**; visibly no regression. Candidates are the other commits between the old `play` build and today, among others the targeted re-translation on unreadable `s_load` (110; one failed entry blocked up to 127 draws, and the figure is skinned by such VS, cf. 96) [INFERENCE].    128. **New requirement (user): launcher EXE with game selection, settings and cheats (task; nothing evidenced yet):** the finished game starts via `bblauncher` – selection of the game (own dump/eboot, check like `bbinstall`), settings (FPS, window/resolution, internal resolution, upscaler, interpolation, save folder) and cheats; the launcher writes `launcher.ini` and starts `bbgame` with the matching environment variables. **Limitations:** `bbgame` is compiled from exactly one eboot – a "game" is the pair (dump, matching `bbgame`); if a matching build is missing, the launcher says so and points to the README build steps. **Cheats:** the user now wants them player-side in the launcher (before: development only, not in the README); they are carried into normal builds as module `BB_CHEATS=god,nohit,stamina,ammo`, act only with a known memory layout (EU 1.00; otherwise "unavailable on this build" without writing); `tp`/`goto`/telemetry stay `BB_DEV_TOOLS`. Tasks: agents CheatsModule and LauncherGui (SDL3 + Dear ImGui, MIT, FetchContent).

   138. **Repository text is English only; tiling hunk measured against a noise baseline (evidence: `build/tb_old2|old3|new2|new3` (`.ppm`, `.log`), `build/en_tr/verify.py`):** user decision: every text in the repository is English (conversation with the user stays German). `docs/ROADMAP.md`, `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` and `manifests/README.md` were translated line for line by subagents (11 ROADMAP chunks, one per agent); automatic checks per line: line count, backtick count, digit runs (separators ignored), list/table markers, `[INFERENCE]` markers; no German prose or umlaut is left (the two Windows dialog titles are given by their English names); decimal commas and space/dot thousands separators were converted (a source `2,000 flips per tick` meaning 2.0 was written as `2.0`). Residual risk: number-format conversions are only checked format-agnostically, so a thousands/decimal mix-up would not be caught; the coordinate-like spots `(0,4,−8)`, `(900,270)`, `(11, 116)`, `(1100,205)` were kept as written. **Tiling hunk (commit 89899ca):** pad-time clinic frame at t = 140, 220 s runs, same arguments (`BB_PRIO=normal BB_PAD_CLOCK=flips BB_SHOT_CLOCK=pad`): pre-change exe (`play\bbgame.exe`, built 17:17) means 14.03 / 13.62, current exe 13.85 / 13.60 (earlier dev runs: 13.90 old, 13.54 new). The same exe differs between runs by up to 0.41 in the mean and 1.48 % of pixels (> 4); old-vs-new pairs lie in the same range (0.63–2.28 %). **No effect of the change is measurable**, so the hunk stays; its VRAM saving (64 MB) remains unmeasured.

Validation is virtual (no copy) against `manifests/CUSA03173-01.09.sha256`: real base `Bloodborne EU` + `bbtools_local/eu_update`: 28844/28844 verified in 20 s (measured twice, independently); merged set 28,404 base + 405 update files (404 replace; the 405th is `sce_discmap_patch.plt`) = 28,809 application files, plus 35 Sc0 system files = 28,844 manifest entries. A real merge install into a scratch folder: 28,812 installed files, post-copy verification 28812/28812, identical to `bb109/Image0` except the three Sc0 files that install maps into `sce_sys` (`param.sfo`, `icon0.png`, `trophy/trophy00.trp`), eboot SHA-256 d65f0b4f… = sidecar of `bbgame_eu109.exe`; the launcher `--dry-run` on that folder selected `bbgame_eu109.exe`; the scratch folder was deleted. A wrong update (the DLC folder) gives clear errors (title mismatch, version unknown, no application files). The modified update `Bloodborne Update 1.09 with 1080p60fps patch.pkg` (title CUSA00900, 13,223 application files, eboot SHA-256 7ca6879e…) extracts fine but is rejected with 'Update folder is not for this game (title CUSA00900 vs CUSA03173)'; no build matches its eboot. **PKG pickers:** the Add dialog takes a game PKG, a DLC PKG and an update PKG and runs an EXTERNAL extractor that the user configures (argv template with `{pkg}`/`{out}`, no shell, no console window, process-tree kill on Cancel, work folder `<data_dir>/pkg_work/<run>` owned by the launcher with a marker, deletable per folder with confirmation, logs kept outside the work folder); the launcher reads only the plain PKG header (content id, title id). The repository still contains no keys and no decryption (README line 6, `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` rewritten accordingly). Real PKG evidence: the header reader showed `EP9000-CUSA03173_00-BLOODBORNE0000EU` (game), `UP9000-CUSA00900_00-SPEXPANSIONDLC03` (DLC) and `EP9000-CUSA03173_00-BLOODBORNE0000EU` (update) for the user's three PKGs; the update PKG (`...EUR-Update-v1.09-PRELUDE.pkg`) and the DLC PKG were extracted through the launcher with the user's local helper (`python …/fpkg_extract.py {pkg} {out}`) in 3 s: the result equals `bbtools_local/eu_update` (436/436 files, 0 content differences) and `eu_dlc` (4/4).         **Corrections (lead, after review; the first version of this item in commit `4f9e9fb` claimed more):** (a) the bridge rise was not observed, and the p32 span state was never tested at the span; (b) the Mergo walk starts from a state after the `ka2` kill, and mf4 starts after `tp` hops, so they are research or not DoD-grade evidence; (c) the first version's "first real hits" was too strong, mf4 ended at 4186/5250 with no kill; (d) at ATK 108 the formula gives 11.9–14.3 per landed R1 (`dmg_report.md`), so a full kill from 5250 HP needs 367–441 landed hits; at the observed mf4 mean of 11.44 it needs about 459, and from the mf4 end state (4186 HP) about 366 more. At the burst-2 rate of 0.45 hits/s that is about 1020 s and 810 s of contact. The 560 s search before the first hit and the blacklist/fallback cycles must be fixed first.
- HLE ABI rule (SysV): a narrow guest parameter (`int`/`short`/`char`/`bool`) arrives with **undefined upper register bits** – every HLE function
  must truncate it when reading (`arg32(c,i)`/`argi(c,i)`/`arg16`/`arg8` from `hle.h`); `wrap<>` does this via the parameter type. The hand-written `fd` / counter / index arguments
  (VFS, Gnm submits, RegisterBuffers) are truncated accordingly; check new HLE functions against the real prototype.
- Debugging lessons: `last HLE import` is the *last import of the thread*, not the fault location. `bb::rt::ld/st` become real
  `memcpy` calls under `/Od` – a fault in a system DLL is therefore a guest load/store; `bad_access`
  (trace build, access < 64 KiB) names it directly. HLE calls are cached (`hle_cache`) because imports such as
  `_Getptolower` run millions of times.
- Note: omitting separate `DT_INIT`/`DT_INIT_ARRAY` in `run_entry` is load-bearing (`_start` runs the inits) and must
  not be "repaired"; thread handles/HLE objects must stay in the low arena.
- Key findings: pointers that HLE hands to the guest program must be < 2^40 (the engine masks `scePthreadSelf()`
  to 40 bits, mutex owner comparison), hence `guest_heap` + `host_map` from 64 GiB; `wchar_t` is 16 bit; libc ctype tables
  per Dinkumware (`_Getpctype`).
- Debug tools (`BB_TRACE_CALLS` build, enabled in `build_full`): `BB_STUB_IMPORTS=1` (log missing imports and return 0
  as result, summary at the end), `BB_TRACE_HLE`, `BB_TRACE_IO`, `BB_TRACE_FN=<vaddr>` (arguments/return value of a
  function), `BB_WATCH=<addr>` (write accesses), `BB_HANG_SECS=<s>` (thread dump with host RIP, link map resolvable),
  `BB_DUMP`, `BB_STR`, `BB_CHAIN`; crash report with guest registers and rbp backtrace.

### 1.4 Trace diffing
- Run shadPS4 with import logging (`log_filter` on `Lib.*:Trace`) on the same dump and produce the same list
  from our HLE log. A diff script shows the first deviation (function, arguments, return value).
- **Acceptance criterion:** The first 10,000 import calls match (modulo time values/handles).

### 1.5 Side paths
- `sceVideoOut` stub with SDL3 window (black image), so that the boot does not hang on flip waits.
- Network/PSN/Trophy/SaveData: offline stubs that report success without data.

## Risks
| Risk | Countermeasure |
|---|---|
| Code volume → compile time of hours | Shard files, unity builds, `-O1` for cold code, profiling to find which functions are hot |
| Self-modifying/JIT code | practically ruled out on PS4 (W^X); still a dispatch fallback with log |
| Unresolved indirect jumps | runtime lookup in the dispatch table, unknown targets → crash with vaddr, add later |
| Havok timing (frame unlock, M5) | Havok stays recompiled; fixed sim tick + render interpolation instead of delta-time patches |
